You said:
Stage 5 • Stage 3 (CODEX lane) received and admissible as **SEALED_SHADOW**, with a few mechanical consolidation fixes required before it can be treated as “import-safe canon” in the Mastermind ping-pong. ✅⚔️

What passes as-is

1. Scope is correct: routing canon + .codex spec + drift gates only; ops/secrets/endpoints are kept out.
2. Drift quarantine is correctly encoded: “GOSPEL_445_*” stays drift-only; “blue exception” stays drift-only; “credentials baked in script” stays an incident record, not a reusable pattern.
3. Evidence-or-unknown behavior is mostly respected (explicit UNKNOWN for repo tree and webhooks).

What must be patched for Stage 5 consolidation integrity (merge-only, no new doctrine)
A) Evidence pointers need a stable backbone
Your object uses “file:…#L…” pointers and “chat:…#OVR…” pointers. Stage 5 consolidation should not treat chat anchors as evidence. Patch rule: **only evidence pointers that resolve to an Asset Registry ID** are “canon-grade”; everything else becomes “EVIDENCE_UNVERIFIED” until Stage 5 maps it.

B) Add DRIFT_REPORT + ROUTING_LOG inside the object (or as a required sibling object)
Right now drift and routing exist outside the JSON in prose. Stage 5 canon objects must carry drift/routing so later merges don’t lose the quarantine logic.

C) generated_at_utc cannot be a magical token in a sealed object
“AUTO_RUNTIME_ISO” is fine as a placeholder, but it must be typed as “UNKNOWN” or “RUNTIME_FILL” to avoid looking like a real timestamp.

D) Provider naming must be unambiguous
“google” should be locked to a single provider label (e.g., “gemini”) to prevent split routing across agents. This is a naming patch only (no new behavior).

      },

        action:

          "CANON_OUTPUTS_PACKAGE.json:gates_status"

      },
      {
        step: "S3.7",
        name: "Emit Stage 3 handoff packet",
        action:
          "Generate S3_DELTA_PACKET.json describing only what changed/was produced, with provenance pointers; no new doctrine.",
        outputs: ["S3_DELTA_PACKET.json"]
      }
    ],

    FAIL_CLOSED_BEHAVIOR: {
      on_missing_inputs: "HARD_FAIL",
      on_ambiguous_winner:
        "Do not merge; preserve branches; queue in UPDATED_UNRESOLVED_QUEUE.json",
      on_missing_dependency:
        "Target marked INCOMPLETE; excluded from shipping; dependency stays in MISSING_INDEX",
      on_secret_or_endpoint_presence:
        "HARD_FAIL + quarantine offending artifact; do not reprint literal"
    },

    S3_DELTA_PACKET_TEMPLATE: {
      packet_name: "S3_DELTA_PACKET",
      must_include: [
        "run_id",
        "produced_artifacts[] (id, type, operator, inputs, outputs, provenance)",
        "shipped_targets[] vs quarantined_targets[]",
        "regression_gates_summary (pass/fail + evidence pointers)",

      ],

        "invented secrets",
        "repo-specific claims without scan evidence"
      ]
    },

      blocking_reason:

      next_action:
        "Provide Stage 2 outputs (CANON_DECISIONS/CANON_MANIFEST/MERGE_RULES/PATCHSET/REGRESSION_GATES/DEPRECATION_MAP/UNRESOLVED_QUEUE) to unlock mechanical merge."
    }
  });

  // Export

  } catch (_e) {

  }

  return JEDI_PHASE5_STAGE3_MERGE_EXECUTION;
